# Skills · T · part 53

909 entries, tennis-surface-form to terminal-diagrams. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **tennis-surface-form**: Use when the user wants to know how a tennis player does on each surface: 'is he better on clay', 'Alcaraz grass record', 'surface splits for Sinner', 'how does she do on hard courts', 'is he a clay courter', 'who is strongest on grass right now'. Compares record, sets and tiebreaks and our Elo by surface and by season, with matches counted. Not for a match preview (see tennis-match-preview), two players' history (see tennis-head-to-head) or a draw simulation (see tennis-tournament-simulation). ([sports-data-hq/sportsdatahq-skills](https://github.com/sports-data-hq/sportsdatahq-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/tennis-data/skills/tennis-surface-form) · ★4)
- **tennis-tournament-simulation**: Use when the user wants title chances for a tennis tournament from the draw: 'who wins Wimbledon', 'simulate the US Open draw', 'chance Sinner reaches the final', 'how tough is Alcaraz's quarter', 'odds of an upset in the first round', 'WTA 1000 draw simulation'. Simulates the draw with our surface Elo ratings, states the method and reports round-by-round probabilities. Not for one match (see tennis-match-preview), a head-to-head record (see tennis-head-to-head) or a finished tournament's results (call get_tournament). ([sports-data-hq/sportsdatahq-skills](https://github.com/sports-data-hq/sportsdatahq-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/tennis-data/skills/tennis-tournament-simulation) · ★4)
